We present AeroEval, an agent-assisted middleware for staged validation of AI-generated drone missions. AeroEval combines deterministic program analysis with context-grounded LLM agents. It first validates program syntax, platform API usage, and mission intent, and then evaluates the realized behavior using execution trajectories, mission requirements, and environmental context. Each stage returns structured failure information for iterative regeneration. In our evaluation using 20 navigation tasks and five analytical mission types over AirSim and Gazebo simulators, AeroEval improves navigation success from 55% to 95%. In a stagewise ablation study, our Code and Trajectory Validators by themselves achieve mean run-level success rates of 44% and 56%, respectively, while the full AeroEval pipeline achieves 88%; the stages detect complementary failures in program structure, API usage, mission intent, obstacle avoidance, altitude, coverage, and event-driven transitions and the guided regeneration corrects for them. Across the main analytics missions, AeroEval increases aggregate run-level success from 34% for one-shot AeroGen to 88% within the regeneration budget. These results demonstrate the benefit of combining program-level and execution-grounded agentic validation for AI-generated drone applications in the evaluated environment.

</details>


### [211] [On-Demand Robotic Assembly via Differentiable Geometric Part Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09777)
*Millicent Schlafly,Fabio Schaub,Diogo Costa Pais,Luca Lelli,Janne Dvorak,Claire Colmont,Sven Marti,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一条端到端的自主流水线，用生成式AI将自然语言提示转化为定制木制结构的3D几何，并通过基于梯度的修复阶段与图注意力网络代理模型优化零件几何，使其适配机器人装配约束，实验证明显著提升可装配率并实现物理演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数字设计到机器人装配目前需要专家数月的manual tuning来协调零件几何与机器人约束，阻碍了按需制造与定制化小批量产品的快速生产。

Method: 生成式AI代理将用户提示翻译为初始3D几何，在设计视觉保真度与部分物理约束间平衡；随后通过基于梯度的修复阶段，在图注意力网络代理模型上反向传播以调整零件几何；除修正不连通与重叠零件外，还进行硬件特定校正，可微优化几何以支持机器人拧螺丝。

Result: 在60个新的自然语言输入中，86.7%可实现机器人拧螺丝，相较先前工作提升十倍；并对其中10个结构用两台UR5e机器人进行了物理装配演示。

Conclusion: 该工作朝着按需机器人制造迈出有意义的一步，可实现定制化、低产量产品的快速生产。

Abstract: Transitioning from a digital design to a robotic assembly process currently requires months of expert manual tuning to reconcile part geometries with robotic constraints. This paper presents an end-to-end, autonomous pipeline for the design and physical construction of bespoke wooden assemblies. A generative AI agent translates user prompts into initial 3D geometries, balancing the visual fidelity of the design with select physical constraints. The assemblability of the design is further improved by a gradient-based repair stage that backpropagates through a graph attention network surrogate to adjust component geometries. In addition to correcting for disjointed and overlapping components, we demonstrate hardware-specific corrections, differentiably optimizing the geometry of components to enable robot screwdriving for 86.7% of 60 novel natural language inputs, significantly outperforming prior work by a factor of ten. For ten of the structures, we physically demonstrate assemblability with two UR5e robots. This work marks a meaningful step toward on-demand robotic manufacturing, enabling the rapid production of customized, low-volume goods.

</details>


### [212] [End-to-End Autonomous Generation of Human Assembly Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09781)
*Faustin Arion von Arx,Millicent Schlafly,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端装配规划方法，将DfA原则编码为成本函数并借助物理仿真与多模态大模型，仅需网格装配即可生成装配序列、工具清单、装配手册与设计反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CAD设计转化为装配计划目前仍主要依赖人工，工程师需权衡几何可行性、工具可达性、稳定性与人因工效等因素，效率低且成本高。

Method: 方法仅输入网格装配体，无需关节元数据、紧固件标注等额外信息。核心包括：在物理仿真器中系统性地拆解物体，并应用编码DfA原则的成本函数进行序列规划；工具的标注与装配手册生成、设计反馈则主要依赖多模态大语言模型，最终输出分步装配手册或结构化失败报告。

Result: 在136个含5至30个零件的装配体上，相比Tian等人"总是先移除最外层零件"的基线，DfA感知的序列规划使模拟装配时间（以机械臂装配时间代理指标衡量）降低35%；装配步骤的工具选择正确率为88.6%；视觉语言模型评判表明生成手册的页面元素确实承载了读者所需信息。

Conclusion: 该方法覆盖装配工具清单、装配序列与子装配、装配手册及可装配性设计反馈四大要素，代码已开源，可供工程师或AI智能体快速加速产品制造计划的生成。

Abstract: Turning a CAD design into an assembly plan is still largely done by hand, requiring engineers to reason about geometric feasibility, tool access, stability, and the ergonomics of human assembly. In this work, we encode long-established design for assembly (DfA) principles into a contained, end-to-end approach for generating assembly plans. Our approach takes only a mesh assembly and produces either a step-by-step assembly manual or a structured failure report, requiring no joint metadata, fastener annotations, or additional information. Four major components of a manufacturing plan are addressed autonomously: an assembly tool list, the assembly sequence and subassemblies, an assembly manual, and design feedback for improving assemblability. For determining the sequence plan, we systematically disassemble the object in a physics simulator and apply a cost function that encodes DfA principles. Manual generation, tool labelling, and assembly feedback rely primarily on multimodal large language models. Compared with a baseline that always removes the outermost part first from Tian et al., DfA-aware sequence planning reduces simulated assembly time, measured with a robot-arm assembly-time proxy, by 35% on 136 assemblies of 5 to 30 parts. The correct tool is selected for 88.6% of assembly steps. A vision-language model judge compares the generated manuals against ablated variants, identifying which page elements carry the information a reader needs. The presented approach and open-source code are available for use by engineers or AI agents looking to rapidly accelerate the creation of manufacturing plans for a given product design.

</details>


### [213] [Enhancing Robotic Perception and Adaptability through Sensor Fusion and Origami-Inspired Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09828)
*Namai Chandra,Jaison Jose,Kavi Arya,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种折纸轮驱动的紧凑移动机器人，通过车轮位姿改变底盘俯仰，从而主动扫描2D LiDAR视角，并与RGB-D深度融合，显著降低无效深度比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑移动机器人在载荷与成本受限下，需在光照变化和无纹理表面上恢复场景几何，但单一传感器视角固定导致深度覆盖不足。

Method: 用折纸结构车轮实现地形自适应运动并主动控制传感几何；车轮运动使底盘俯仰变化，带动2D LiDAR扫描不同仰角；IMU补偿底盘姿态，融合节点将LiDAR回波投影到RGB-D深度流并输入RTAB-Map；复用已有的车轮驱动，成本低于300美元、重量低于2 kg。

Result: 在无纹理室内走廊与阳光照射室外区域各配置下各跑3次：室内平均整帧无效深度比例由21%降至11%，室外由48%降至18%。

Conclusion: 该原型以复用轮式驱动实现连续可调的LiDAR视角，同时提升深度覆盖率，兼顾低成本与轻量化。

Abstract: Compact mobile robots must recover scene geometry under changing lighting and surface texture while working within tight payload and cost limits. We present a compact mobile robot that uses origami-inspired wheels for locomotion and active control of its sensing geometry. As the wheels move between terrain-adaptive configurations, the changing chassis pitch sweeps a 2D LiDAR through intermediate elevations; held wheel positions provide a chosen viewing angle. An IMU accounts for chassis attitude, and a fusion node projects LiDAR returns into the RGB-D depth stream supplied to RTAB-Map. The arrangement uses the wheel actuation already present on a sub-300 USD, sub-2 kg prototype to extend the scanner's viewing geometry. We assess depth fusion in a textureless indoor corridor and an outdoor sunlit area, with three runs per sensor configuration in each setting. Mean full-frame invalid-depth fractions fell from 21% to 11% indoors and from 48% to 18% outdoors. The prototype combines improved depth coverage with a continuously adjustable LiDAR viewpoint using the same actuation that reconfigures its wheels.

</details>


### [214] [Towards Accurate End-Effector Localization for UMI-Style Robotic Manipulation Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09857)
*Junjie Zhang,Deteng Zhang,Zhisong Xu,Bo Sun,Liuyang Li,Yihong Tian,Jie Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MILD操作界面定位数据集与AprilVINS方法，用于机器人示教学习中近距离操作与遮挡下的末端执行器定位评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM基准侧重导航运动，操作数据集侧重策略学习，缺乏对近距离操作、相机遮挡场景下末端执行器定位的评测，导致示教学习的定位精度与时间覆盖无法系统衡量。

Method: 构建MILD数据集，含真实世界86条Insta360 X5与Insight9序列（15类重复桌面任务、标定资源及逐次执行TCP参考轨迹）和Isaac Sim仿真子集MILD-Sim；提出AprilVINS，融合鱼眼视觉惯性估计与序列局部AprilTag几何，将先验准入与受保护导出分离，实现联合优化的状态输出。

Result: 基准测试显示不同视觉惯性/基准标靶方法在TCP相对轨迹误差与时间覆盖上差异显著；在Insta360 AprilTag4数据上，AprilVINS(full)实现毫米级SE(3)对齐TCP相对APE RMSE与高时间完成度，误差低于对比路线，而纯鱼眼VIO仍为厘米级；消融实验分离精度与可导出性，MILD-Sim回放提供任务特定容差参考。

Conclusion: MILD与AprilVINS共同构成面向UMI风格示教采集的诊断性基准框架，代码、数据集与评测清单将在录用后发布。

Abstract: Robot demonstration learning requires accurate and temporally complete end-effector localization during close-range manipulation and camera occlusion. Existing SLAM benchmarks emphasize navigation motions, whereas manipulation datasets prioritize policy learning over localization evaluation. We introduce MILD, a Manipulation-Interface Localization Dataset with real-world and simulation sequences. The real-world subset provides 86 sensor sequences from Insta360 X5 and Insight9 across 15 repeated tabletop tasks, calibration assets, and a per-execution robot end-effector reference trajectory. The simulation subset, MILD-Sim, extends task coverage in Isaac Sim for controlled manipulation-replay studies. Benchmarking visual-inertial and fiducial-aided systems on instrumented real-world recordings reveals large differences in both TCP-relative trajectory error and temporal coverage, even under the same nominal task. To support marker-augmented teaching workspaces without a pre-surveyed fiducial map, we present AprilVINS, which combines fisheye visual-inertial estimation with sequence-local AprilTag geometry and separates prior admission from guarded export of the jointly optimized state. On Insta360 AprilTag4 recordings, AprilVINS(full) under a unified protocol with sequence-specific profiles reaches millimeter-level SE(3)-aligned TCP-relative APE RMSE with high time completion and lower reported error than the tested routes under their respective protocols, whereas fisheye VIO without tag factors remains at centimeter scale. Ablations separate accuracy from exportability, and a MILD-Sim replay study provides task-specific tolerance references for interpreting those error magnitudes. Together, MILD and AprilVINS provide a diagnostic benchmarking framework for UMI-style demonstration collection. Code, datasets, and evaluation manifests will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [215] [Juno: Taming Predictive Latents for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09940)
*Yuchen Zhu,Chenyi Xu,Yulin Zhang,Gang Xu,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Juno提出一个以动作条件化JEPA为核心的统一框架，兼顾控制对齐表征、预测教师与可适应动力学模型，在仿真与真机上显著提升成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA能在表征空间预测掩码或未来观测，为VLA模型提供预测性隐变量，但存在三个问题：与具身特定控制不匹配、干扰动作学习、分布偏移下教师失准。

Method: 预训练阶段在具身匹配轨迹上训练动作条件JEPA，并用动态CLS损失将运动加权patch动力学迁移到紧凑全局状态；策略学习阶段将当前帧JEPA patch融入VLA感知，用解耦推理分支（独立变换参数）蒸馏未来隐状态以生成动作；部署阶段在所有观测转移（含失败rollout）上适应世界模型，冻结适应后的教师，并用LoRA适配器和可训练动作头在验证过的执行上重新对齐策略，无需专家纠正或任务奖励。

Result: 在SimplerEnv上，平均成功率由最强基线Qwen3GR00T的60.9%提升至68.5%，测试时适应进一步达72.7%；在真实机器人上，面对背景、高度和物体变化时保持70%–75%成功率，而基线策略降至0%。

Conclusion: 统一的动作条件JEPA可同时作为控制对齐表征骨干、预测教师与自适应动力学模型，有效解决预训练、策略学习与部署三阶段的失配问题，提升VLA的泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) predict masked or future observations in representation space, offering a natural source of predictive latents for vision-language-action (VLA) models. Yet making these latents useful across pretraining, policy learning, and deployment requires addressing three failures: mismatch with embodiment-specific control, interference with action learning, and teacher miscalibration under distribution shifts. We introduce Juno, a unified framework built around one action-conditioned JEPA that serves as a control-aligned representation backbone, a predictive teacher, and an adaptable dynamics model. During pretraining, we train it on embodiment-matched trajectories and use a dynamic CLS loss to transfer motion-weighted patch dynamics to a compact global state. During policy learning, we fuse current-frame JEPA patches into VLA perception and use a decoupled reasoning branch with separate transformation parameters to distill future latent states for action generation. During deployment, we adapt the world model on all observed transitions, including failed rollouts, freeze the adapted teacher, and re-align the policy on verified executions using LoRA adapters and a trainable action head, without expert corrections or task rewards. On SimplerEnv, Juno raises average success from $60.9\%$ to $68.5\%$ over Qwen3GR00T, the strongest baseline, and test-time adaptation further reaches $72.7\%$; on a real robot, it retains $70\%$--$75\%$ success under background, height, and object shifts where the base policy collapses to $0\%$.

</details>


### [216] [Many Ways to Succeed: Diversity-Driven RL Fine-Tuning for VLA Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09943)
*Haoru Li,Jinmei Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Xiaoming Li,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DRIVE，将成功行为的多样性作为显式 RL 目标，提升 VLA 策略的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 微调可提升 VLA 策略，但超出微调分布后的泛化仍受限。分析发现 RL 会全局收缩行为，但同时增加成功轨迹的多样性、提高成功率并覆盖更多潜在的有效解空间，这可能是应对分布偏移的关键。

Method: DRIVE 将成功行为多样性转为显式 RL 目标：在相同任务条件下对 rollout 分组，做时间对齐后比较轨迹，并从相对行为多样性中导出成功条件下的内在奖励，从而鼓励覆盖更多可行解，同时避免奖励失败或表面的时序差异。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、ManiSkill3 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上，DRIVE 相比 vanilla RL 微调平均 OOD 性能在 π0 上提升 5.3 分、π0.5 上提升 2.0 分；在 AgileX PiPER-X 双臂平台，OOD 成功率从 64.1% 提升至 73.3%（+9.2 分）。

Conclusion: 通过显式优化成功行为的多样性，DRIVE 有效增强了 VLA 策略在分布偏移下的泛化，并在物理部署中保持增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning improves vision-language-action (VLA) policies through closed-loop experience, yet generalization beyond the fine-tuning distribution remains limited. Our analysis reveals a selective reshaping of exploration: RL contracts behavior globally, yet diversifies successful trajectories, elicits success with fewer rollouts, and covers more of the latent task-valid solution space than supervised fine-tuning. Broader successful-mode coverage may provide alternative strategies under distribution shifts. Inspired by this, we introduce DRIVE (Diversity-driven RL fIne-tuning for VLA gEneralization), which turns successful-behavior diversity into an explicit RL objective. DRIVE groups rollouts under matched task conditions, compares their trajectories with temporal alignment, and derives a success-conditioned intrinsic reward from relative behavioral diversity. This design encourages broader coverage of feasible solutions without rewarding diverse failures or superficial timing differences. Across LIBERO-Plus, ManiSkill3, and RoboTwin 2.0, DRIVE improves the average out-of-domain (OOD) performance over vanilla RL fine-tuning by 5.3 points on $π_0$ and 2.0 points on $π_{0.5}$. On a dual-arm AgileX PiPER-X platform, DRIVE further increases average OOD success from 64.1% to 73.3% (+9.2 points), demonstrating gains that persist under physical deployment.

</details>


### [217] [Borrowed Eyes: Markerless Nano-UAV Flight with an Active Quadruped Observer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09967)
*Alejandro Lorite Mora,Dimitrios Arapis,Andrés Faíña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 四足机器人搭载机械臂相机，通过视觉分割与深度估计对纳米无人机定位，并用感知感知NMPC动态调整身体与机械臂保持视线，实现中位3D定位误差59 mm的起降与轨迹飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机可进入大型机器人无法通行的狭小空间，但其载荷能力严重限制了可用于自定位的传感器与算力，尤其在GNSS拒止环境下难以自主定位。

Method: 四足机器人用臂载相机跟踪无人机，基于分割掩码与深度估计其在自身坐标系中的位置，并通过实时无线链路传输给无人机；为保持持续跟踪，设计了感知感知的非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC），将身体与机械臂的运动动态调整，把观测可靠性作为主要控制目标。

Result: 仅依赖外部估计，纳米无人机完成了从起飞到着陆的预设轨迹，中位3D定位误差为59 mm；在短暂视觉遮挡导致视线丢失时，可平滑切换到机载惯性飞行，体现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架使四足机器人能够卸载纳米无人机的定位负担，从而实现对单个机器人无法导航的复杂空间进行巡检。

Abstract: Nano unmanned aerial vehicles (nano-UAVs) can navigate confined spaces that larger robots cannot, but their payload capacity severely limits the sensors and compute available for self-localization in global navigation satellite system (GNSS)-denied environments. We present a vision-based system that localizes a nano-UAV from a quadruped robot with an arm-mounted camera. The quadruped tracks the drone, estimates its position in its own coordinate system using segmentation masks and depth, and transmits that position over a real-time radio link. To ensure continuous tracking, we developed a perception-aware nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) that dynamically adjusts the quadruped's body and arm to maximize the drone's visibility, treating observation reliability as a primary control objective. Relying solely on this external estimate, the nano-UAV executed predefined trajectories from takeoff to landing with a median 3D localization error of 59 mm. The system demonstrates robustness against short visual occlusions by smoothly transitioning to onboard inertial flight when line-of-sight is temporarily lost. Ultimately, this framework allows a quadruped to offload the localization burden of a nano-UAV, enabling inspection of complex spaces that neither robot could navigate alone.

</details>


### [218] [Decoding Neural Population Dynamics through Robotic Analog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09977)
*Wenhui Chen,Jiyue Tao,Yitao Cheng,Yutong Shi,Feitian Zhang,Xitong Liang,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了带人工肌肉、多模态传感器和强化学习神经网络控制器的机器人模型，复现了动物运动皮层中的旋转神经群动力学，揭示了神经旋转与运动结果之间的因果联系，并发现了优化轨迹的振荡动作、神经能量原则及学习中的"顿悟"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物证据表明精确的自主动作源自运动皮层中旋转的神经群动力学，但其物理效应尚不清楚。作者希望弥合生物系统与人工系统之间的鸿沟，探究神经动力学与运动结果之间的因果关系。

Method: 开发了一个生物运动系统的机器人模拟体，包含人工肌肉、多模态传感器，以及通过强化学习训练的神经网络控制器。

Result: 该机器人模拟体表现出精确的动作、对损伤的鲁棒性以及与动物相似的神经群动力学；发现神经旋转产生了与伸手方向正交的振荡动作，从而优化轨迹调整，并由灵长类神经数据证实；还揭示了传感器与运动冗余下的反直觉神经能量原则，以及运动学习过程中显著的"顿悟时刻"。

Conclusion: 这些发现为神经动力学如何促成精确而灵活的运动提供了新视角，可启发未来具备类动物运动能力的智能机器人。

Abstract: Animal evidence shows that precise voluntary movements arise from rotational neural population dynamics in motor cortex, but their physical effects remain unknown. We developed a robotic analog of biological motor systems with artificial muscles, multimodal sensors, and a neural network controller trained via reinforcement learning. The robotic analog exhibited accurate movements, robustness to damage, and neural population dynamics akin to animals. This task-driven, embodied model illuminates the causal link between neural population dynamics and motor outcomes. We discovered that neural rotations generate oscillatory maneuvers orthogonal to the reaching direction, optimizing trajectory adjustments, which is confirmed by primate neural data. The model also revealed counterintuitive neural energy principles under sensor and motor redundancies, and striking Eureka moments during motor learning, bridging biological and artificial systems. These findings provide new perspectives on how neural dynamics contribute to accurate and flexible movement, inspiring future intelligent robots with animal-like mobility.

</details>


### [219] [Tactile Reconstruction of Contact Task Frames and Forces for Hybrid Force/Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10020)
*Antonio Rapuano,Simone Orelli,Barbara Bazzana,Antonio Franchi,Alessandro De Luca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出仅用软光触觉传感在线估计接触力与任务坐标系的方法，通过自标注学习将单张形变图像映射为接触变量，并结合EKF与机器人本体感知实现闭环力调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合力/运动控制需要交互力与任务坐标系知识，传统方法依赖力/力矩传感或模型残差，且常需名义环境模型，限制了无外部力传感场景下的应用。

Method: 在局部平面接触且接触力矩可忽略假设下，将软光触觉传感器弹性体单张形变图像映射为可观测接触变量：压入深度、两个表面-传感器倾角及3D接触力，并给出逐样本不确定性；通过自标注采集流程，由机械臂施加受控接触、辅助力/力矩传感器离线提供真值标签进行学习；再将触觉测量与机器人本体感知在扩展卡尔曼滤波中融合，持续估计任务坐标系与交互力。

Result: 在UR10机械臂上搭载DigiTac传感器进行控制实验，仅以触觉作为外部感知反馈，实现对处在线性与角运动中的平面刚性板的闭环接触力调节。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅凭软光触觉传感即可在线估计接触力与时变任务坐标系，并支撑稳定的闭环力控制，验证了该自标注学习与EKF融合框架的有效性。

Abstract: Hybrid force/motion control requires knowledge of the interaction force and of a task frame defining the force- and motion-controlled directions. These quantities are usually obtained from force/torque sensing or model-based residuals, often assuming also a nominal environment model. This work addresses the online estimation of the contact force and a possibly time-varying task frame using only soft optical tactile sensing, under the assumption of locally planar contact with a negligible contact moment. The proposed method maps a single image of the deformed elastomer of a soft optical tactile sensor to observable contact variables: indentation depth, two surface-to-sensor tilt angles, and 3D contact force, each with a per-sample uncertainty estimate. The mapping is learned through a self-labeling acquisition procedure, in which a manipulator imposes controlled contacts while an auxiliary Force/Torque sensor is used offline to provide ground-truth labels. The tactile measurement is then fused with robot proprioceptive data in an Extended Kalman Filter, producing a continuously updated estimate of the contact task frame and of the interaction force. Control experiments with a DigiTac sensor mounted on a UR10 manipulator demonstrate closed-loop contact force regulation against a flat rigid board in linear and angular motion by a human operator, with touch as the only exteroceptive feedback.

</details>


### [220] [Video-to-Model: Automatic Modeling of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10039)
*Akshun Sharma,Kimia Forghani,Yancy Diaz-Mercado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从视频自动建模可变形缝合线运动的视频到模型框架，利用CBF-CLF-QP数值模型和时空CNN估计有效参数，实验证明能低误差重建线体行为并减少人工调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线状物体（如缝合线）的运动建模通常需要繁琐的人工参数调节，缺乏从视频自动建模的手段，本文旨在减少手动调参并提供视频到模型的自动化途径。

Method: 采用CBF-CLF-QP数值模型，通过少量参数表征可变形线运动；感知模块先定位并跟踪视频中的线，生成有序节点序列；再由时空CNN网络从观测运动中估计结构化模型的有效参数；最后在用户定义的针速度输入下仿真线程运动。

Result: 在未见过的线配置和运动实验中，框架能可靠从视频重建线行为，自动配置结构化模型，并以低跟踪误差复现预期线运动。

Conclusion: 该方法减少了人工参数调优需求，是迈向基于视频的自动可变形线性物体建模的一步。

Abstract: This paper presents a video-to-model framework for automatically modeling the motion of a deformable suture thread from an input video. We utilize a recently developed CBF--CLF--QP numerical model that simplifies the characterization of deformable string motion through the selection of a small number of parameters. A perception module first localizes and tracks the thread in video, producing an ordered sequence of thread nodes. The observed thread motion is then processed by a spatio-temporal CNN network that estimates the effective parameters of a structured CBF--CLF--QP model. These parameters are used to simulate the thread under a user-defined needle velocity input. Experiments using unseen thread configurations and motion demonstrate that the framework can reliably reconstruct the thread behavior from video, automatically configure the structured model, and reproduce the expected thread motion with low tracking error. The proposed approach reduces the need for manual parameter tuning and provides a step toward automatic video-based modeling of deformable linear objects.

</details>


### [221] [Distributed Motion Planning for Multi-Robot Systems under Topological Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10065)
*Gianpietro Battocletti,Dimitris Boskos,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型预测控制（MPC）的分布式控制器，利用编织的绕数作为代理，实现对多机器人编织拓扑约束的高效执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协调中，拓扑编织约束虽能紧凑表达复杂协调模式，但现有分布式执行方法通常一次只执行一个编织生成元，导致轨迹缓慢且次优，执行效率低。

Method: 用绕数（编织的拓扑不变量）代替直接跟踪编织规范：一是将编织转化为可由MPC代价函数项追踪的连续函数，二是将全局编织规范解耦为成对规范，通过求解局部MPC问题分布式跟踪；并采用基于共识的进度估计使机器人同步运动。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中验证了方法的有效性，在执行速度和控制代价方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 基于MPC与绕数代理的分布式控制框架能够高效执行编织拓扑规范，实现机器人间的全局协调，性能较现有方法更优。

Abstract: Efficient and distributed coordination of mobile robots is one of the main challenges in multi-robot systems. Topological constraints, often expressed as topological braids, are a popular tool to encode complex coordination patterns between multiple mobile robots, as they offer a compact and abstract representation of the desired qualitative relation between the space-time trajectories of the robots. However, execution of joint motion plans encoded as braid-based topological constraints via distributed controllers is challenging, with existing approaches, generally based on the execution of one braid generator at a time, producing slow and suboptimal trajectories. We propose a distributed controller based on Model Predictive Control (MPC) to efficiently execute braid-based topological specifications. Rather than directly tracking the braid specification, we propose to use winding numbers, which are topological invariants for braids, as a proxy. This has the twofold benefit of converting braids into a continuous function, which can be easily tracked by an MPC controller through an appropriate term in the cost function, and of decoupling the global braid specification into a set of pairwise specifications, which can be tracked distributedly through the solution of only local MPC problems. To maintain global coordination, we propose a consensus-based progress estimation approach, which allows the robots to synchronize their motion toward the desired specification. We validate the proposed approach in simulation and in real-world experiments, where we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and the improvement over existing approaches in terms of execution speed and control effort.

</details>


### [222] [RealtimeWAM: How Fast Can I Run My World Action Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10079)
*Huanan Liu,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Xiaoyu Chen,Hanyun Cui,Yutian Shen,Yuan Meng,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Bo Li,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RealtimeWAM，一个通用、免训练框架，通过并行执行与自适应计算协同，实现多种 WAM 架构的低延迟推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 结合视觉动态建模与动作生成，但推理延迟高，限制机器人实时控制。FastWAM 需专门架构设计，而通用缓存策略仅利用特征冗余，无法应对闭环控制中变化的计算需求。

Method: 利用层级依赖将观测处理与预测重叠执行；针对并发分支竞争 GPU 资源的问题，采用选择性复用进行自适应计算，缓存视觉稳定区域的观测特征，复用 Transformer 残差，并对小幅预测调整保留额外细化。

Result: 在 FastWAM 和 OpenWAM 上于 RoboTwin、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 评测。RTX 4090 上平均推理延迟为 24.09 ms 和 63.09 ms，平均加速 8.90× 和 10.67×；平均成功率 82.75% 和 87.41%，与原生推理相差 0.02 和 0.53 个百分点。五个真实任务上成功率分别提升 17.2 和 37.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM 作为免训练通用框架，能在保持成功率的同时大幅降低 WAM 推理延迟，并在真实任务中提升表现，具有较好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine visual dynamics modeling with action generation, but their high inference latency limits responsive robot control. Recent efforts accelerate inference by removing explicit future-video generation at test time, as in FastWAM, an approach that requires a specially tailored architectural design. More general caching strategies exploit feature redundancy, but redundancy alone does not capture the changing computational demands of closed-loop control. To address these challenges, we present RealtimeWAM, a general, training-free framework that coordinates parallel execution with adaptive computation for low-latency inference across diverse WAM architectures. We exploit layerwise dependencies to overlap observation processing with prediction. However, concurrent branches still compete for GPU resources, limiting the benefit of parallel execution. We therefore adapt computation throughout the pipeline through selective reuse, caching observation features in visually stable regions and reusing Transformer residuals while reserving additional refinement for small predicted adjustments. We evaluate RealtimeWAM on FastWAM and OpenWAM across RoboTwin, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus. On an RTX 4090, measured mean inference latencies are 24.09 and 63.09 ms, corresponding to average speedups of 8.90$\times$ and 10.67$\times$. Average success rates are 82.75% and 87.41%, respectively, within 0.02 and 0.53 percentage points of native inference. Across five real-world tasks, RealtimeWAM improves average success rates over native inference by 17.2 and 37.2 percentage points on FastWAM and OpenWAM, respectively.

</details>


### [223] [Lifelong small-object navigation in changing object layouts: a benchmark and method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10125)
*Jiagan Huang,Zikun Zhou,Zijian Ni,Hongpeng Wang,Guangming Lu,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"终身小物体导航"新任务LiSoNav-COL，构建基准LiSoNav-Eval，并提出基于多视角检查与视点锚定记忆的方法IVAM-Nav，在实验中表现优于代表性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需在同一环境中反复导航至不同物体，其中许多是小而便携的物体（如工具、玩具）。这些物体视觉占位小、易被遮挡，且可能被人移动而机器人未察觉，导致可靠观察与定位困难。同时，现有任务缺乏针对"无需预先扫描、从空记忆开始、需复用与更新场景知识"这些核心假设的专门基准。

Method: 1) 形式化定义LiSoNav-COL任务：智能体需寻找合适视角以可靠观察小物体，积累并复用场景知识以高效定位后续目标，并在物体被移动后更新过时记忆；要求以空场景记忆开始导航。2) 构建LiSoNav-Eval基准：覆盖28个室内场景、45个小物体类别，其终身导航序列包含未变动与已迁移目标。3) 提出IVAM-Nav方法：基于多视角检查与视点锚定记忆，主动从互补视角观察支撑表面以实现可靠的小物体感知，并将记忆锚定到观察视点，支持在相似观察条件下的关系型记忆复用与再验证。

Result: 在LiSoNav-Eval上的大量实验表明，IVAM-Nav相比代表性方法取得了良好性能。基准分析还显示：物体越小、环境越大、物体迁移距离越长，任务挑战越大。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了终身小物体导航任务在基准方面的空白，提出LiSoNav-Eval基准与IVAM-Nav方法，验证了多视角观察与视点锚定记忆在应对小物体、动态布局变化时的有效性，并揭示了影响任务难度的关键因素。

Abstract: Household robots need to continually navigate to different objects in the same environment, many of which are small and portable, such as tools and toys. Their small visual footprint and frequent occlusion make reliable observation difficult, and they may be moved by people without the robot observing the changes. We formulate this challenging task as Lifelong Small-object Navigation in Changing Object Layouts (LiSoNav-COL). Agents must seek suitable viewpoints for reliable observation, accumulate and reuse scene knowledge to efficiently locate subsequent targets, and update outdated memory after object relocation. To eliminate the need for prior scene scanning, we also require agents to start navigation with empty scene memory. Although practical, this task still lacks benchmarks designed around its defining assumptions. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiSoNav-Eval, a dedicated benchmark spanning 28 indoor scenes with 45 small-object categories. Its lifelong navigation sequences include both unchanged and relocated targets to evaluate memory reuse and adaptation to object relocation. To address this challenging task, we propose a navigation method based on multi-view Inspection with Viewpoint-Anchored Memory, dubbed IVAM-Nav. IVAM-Nav actively observes supporting surfaces from complementary viewpoints for reliable small-object perception and anchors the resulting memory to their observation viewpoints, supporting relational memory reuse and revalidation under similar viewing conditions. Extensive experiments on LiSoNav-Eval demonstrate favorable performance of IVAM-Nav against representative methods. Benchmark analyses also show that smaller objects, larger environments, and longer relocation distances pose greater challenges. The dataset and code are available here.

</details>


### [224] [Do Vision-Language-Action Models Understand Instructions? A Mechanistic Interpretability Study on Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10178)
*Theodor Wulff,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过激活修补与归因修补，考察了两个先进VLA模型（π_0.5与GR00T N1.7）对语言指令的依赖程度，发现两者对抽象改写与虚构物体不敏感，但对空指令尤其方向性语言高度敏感，且敏感性所在层位与归因修补可靠性均因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型被期望能跨环境与任务描述泛化，但其动作生成是否真正依赖语言指令，还是主要依赖视觉线索与表层相关性，尚不明确。由于VLA缺乏显式的语言接地模块，只能依赖VLM骨干的固有接地能力，而对视觉与语言观测空间变化的鲁棒性对实际部署至关重要，因此需要机制层面的可控研究。

Method: 在LIBERO基准上，对两个SOTA模型π_0.5和GR00T N1.7的动作生成模块残差流施加激活修补与归因修补。采用五种指令破坏策略：同义词替换、语义缩放、方向性破坏、随机物体替换和空字符串，系统性地扰动任务指令，并观察因果效应与内部表征几何的变化。

Result: 两个模型对抽象改写和引用不存在的物体相对不敏感，但对空任务描述、尤其是方向性语言反应强烈。敏感性在动作生成中集中于不同位置：GR00T N1.7主要在早期周期性交叉注意力层，π_0.5则分布在最早层与部分较后层。对GR00T N1.7，方向性扰动产生最大的因果效应之一，却几乎不改变内部表征几何，这种分离在π_0.5中不明显。归因修补的可靠性依模型而异：对GR00T N1.7与激活修补高度一致，对π_0.5则不一致。

Conclusion: VLA模型的语言接地并不均匀：其对方向性语言与空指令高度敏感，而对抽象改写与虚构物体不敏感；敏感层位与因果-表征分离现象因模型架构而异；归因修补作为激活修补近似手段的有效性也是模型相关的，使用时需谨慎验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are designed to generalise across environments and task descriptions, raising the question of whether their action generation actually depends on the language instruction, or whether they largely rely on visual cues and superficial correlations. Robustness to variance in the visual and linguistic observation space is critical for real-world deployment, yet VLAs lack explicit grounding modules and instead rely on the intrinsic language grounding capabilities of their Vision-Language model backbones. For this reason, we conduct a controlled mechanistic interpretability study on the language grounding capabilities of two state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action models, $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7, by applying activation and attribution patching to the residual stream of the action generation modules. We systematically corrupt the task instruction of input samples of the LIBERO benchmark following five strategies: synonym replacement, semantic scaling, directional corruption, random object substitution, and empty string. Our experiments find that both models are comparatively insensitive to abstract rephrasing and to referencing non-existent objects, but react strongly to empty task descriptions and, especially, to directional language. During action generation, this sensitivity is concentrated in different loci for each model: mainly in the early, periodic cross-attention layers for GR00T N1.7, versus distributed across the earliest and selected later layers for $π_{0.5}$. For GR00T N1.7, directional perturbations drive some of the largest causal effects while leaving the internal representational geometry comparatively unchanged, a dissociation we do not observe clearly for $π_{0.5}$. Finally, the reliability of attribution patching is model-dependent: it closely tracks activation patching for GR00T N1.7 but not for $π_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [225] [Design of a Fully Actuated 4-DOF Robotic Finger With Joint-Specific Hybrid Remote Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10180)
*Hyojae Kang,Hyun-mok Jung,Joonho Lee,Dongil Park,Hyunmin Do,Jongwoo Park,Jeongdo Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全驱动4自由度机器人手指，采用关节特定的混合远程驱动架构，实现MCP、PIP、DIP关节的解耦驱动与力输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升机器人手指的全驱动能力与关节解耦性能，需要一种兼顾紧凑传动与独立控制的驱动架构。

Method: MCP关节由两组协调的刚性连杆传动驱动；PIP与DIP关节由含圆形滚动接触关节（RCJ）的闭环线传动独立驱动。PIP传动半径大于DIP，RCJ线几何在关节旋转时保持线环总长不变，且DIP走线设计使其差动驱动不受PIP运动影响。对远端线传动、MCP连杆与指尖运动学进行了建模，并用ArUco标记跟踪滚珠丝杠位移进行实验评估。

Result: MCP驱动会引起PIP和DIP传动单元的可测位移；在MCP固定时，单独驱动PIP和DIP支持远端传动间的机械解耦。MCP、PIP、DIP单独驱动下的指尖峰值力分别为21.28 N、9.22 N和5.75 N；并用不同几何与尺寸的物体考察了手指姿态。

Conclusion: 该混合远程驱动架构可实现4自由度手指的全驱动与远端关节解耦，并具备可观的指尖力输出与多样抓取姿态适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully actuated 4-DOF robotic finger using a joint-specific hybrid remote-actuation architecture. The metacarpophalangeal (MCP) joint is driven by two coordinated rigid-link transmission sets, whereas the proximal interphalangeal (PIP) and distal interphalangeal (DIP) joints are independently actuated by closed-loop wire transmissions incorporating circular rolling-contact joints (RCJs). A larger transmission radius is used at the PIP joint than at the DIP joint. The RCJ wire geometry maintains the total wire-loop length during joint rotation, and the DIP wire routing is designed so that PIP motion does not affect its differential actuation for a fixed MCP configuration. The distal wire transmission, MCP linkage, and fingertip kinematics are analytically modeled. In experiments, the transmission behavior was quantitatively evaluated from the ball-screw displacements measured using ArUco-marker tracking. MCP actuation produced measurable displacements of the PIP and DIP transmission units, whereas isolated PIP and DIP actuation with the MCP fixed supported the intended mechanical decoupling between the distal transmissions. The mean peak fingertip forces under isolated MCP, PIP, and DIP actuation were 21.28 N, 9.22 N, and 5.75 N, respectively. The resulting finger postures were also examined using objects of different geometries and sizes.

</details>


### [226] [Benchmarking Behavioral Steerability in Behavior Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10198)
*Minghe Gao,Zhanxi Yan,Jiahui Liu,Wendong Bu,Xiaoting Chen,Qizhou Wang,Yi Su,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Tat-Seng Chua,Juncheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"行为可引导性"概念并构建首个评测基准 RoboSteer，用于衡量行为基础模型能否忠实生成符合用户意图的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（BFMs）正从行为生成走向通用行为系统，一个根本问题随之出现：它们能否被可靠地按用户意图引导？目前缺乏对该能力的系统研究与评测手段。

Method: 引入"行为可引导性"概念（BFMs 忠实生成满足用户指定意图行为的能力）；提出 RoboSteer 基准，将行为可引导性组织为三层级——条件引导、约束引导与组合引导，并基于大规模多模态运动语料库建立统一评测框架。

Result: 利用 RoboSteer 对 9 个现有 BFMs 开展了首次大规模行为可引导性实证研究。

Conclusion: 行为可引导性不仅是运动控制能力，更关乎具身系统如何将人类意图转化为有目的的行动；期望 RoboSteer 推动"意图实现"研究，成为通用具身智能的基础。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) are emerging as a paradigm for translating human intentions into executable humanoid behaviors. As these models evolve beyond behavior generation toward general-purpose behavioral systems, a fundamental question arises: can they be reliably steered according to user intentions? In this paper, we introduce the concept of behavioral steerability, defined as the ability of BFMs to faithfully generate behaviors that satisfy user-specified intentions. To study this capability, we present RoboSteer, the first benchmark for behavioral steerability in BFMs. RoboSteer organizes behavioral steerability into a three-level hierarchy-Conditional Steering, Constraint Steering, and Compositional Steering-and establishes a unified evaluation framework supported by a large-scale multimodal motion corpus. Using RoboSteer, we conduct the first large-scale empirical study of behavioral steerability across 9 existing BFMs. We view behavioral steerability as more than a capability for controlling motion: it concerns how embodied systems translate human intentions into purposeful actions. We hope RoboSteer will advance research on intention realization as a foundation for general-purpose embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [227] [Making Task Abstractions Executable: Control-Aware Layout Repair for a Fixed Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10281)
*Chiyoung Kim,Seungyeon Back,Sumin Shim,Doyoung Heo,Rita Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将任务抽象中的布局约束编译为可审计仿射约束的方法，仅修复连续坐标以保持事件顺序、时序、拓扑和控制器不变，并在三个任务抽象上验证了两种后端均能通过认证并完成全部配对 rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务抽象虽可指定预期事件，但其空间布局可能使固定智能体与控制器无法完成任务，因此需要一种仅通过调整布局即可满足全任务需求的方法。

Method: 从结构化任务记录出发，将全任务跟踪、间隙和驱动需求编译为可审计的仿射布局约束；仅修复声明的连续坐标，保持事件顺序、时序、拓扑与控制器；采用最违反行更新实现条件有限认证与净位移界；用同编译器二次投影将表示与优化器分离。

Result: 在三个研究者编写的任务抽象上，两种后端均认证全部三种布局，并完成每种后端 300 次全新配对 rollout；风险目标扫描还暴露出仅靠布局编辑无法满足的固定事件测试。

Conclusion: 该方法能将布局修复形式化为可审计约束问题，在保持任务语义与控制器的前提下实现认证与优化分离，但也揭示了部分固定事件测试无法仅通过布局编辑满足的局限。

Abstract: A task abstraction can specify the intended events while its spatial layout prevents a fixed agent and controller from completing them. Starting from a supplied structured task record, we compile whole-task tracking, clearance, and actuation requirements into auditable affine layout constraints. We repair only declared continuous coordinates, preserving event order, timing, topology, and the controller. A most-violated-row update admits conditional finite-certification and net-displacement bounds; a same-compiler quadratic projection separates the representation from the optimizer. On three researcher-authored task abstractions, both backends certify all three layouts and complete all 300 fresh paired rollouts per backend. A risk-target sweep also exposes fixed event tests that the chosen certificate cannot satisfy through layout edits alone.

</details>


### [228] [Temporal Visuo-Tactile Learning for Dexterous Grasp Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10283)
*Ken Nakahara,Aleksei Buvailik,Prokhor Kotov,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究高分辨率动态触觉传感对灵巧手抓取稳定性预测与模型引导抓取的作用，构建1万次抓取试验数据集并训练时序多模态模型，显示触觉（尤其高分辨率动态触觉）显著提升稳定性预测，并在真机上以视触觉门控将执行抓取成功率提升10.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类依靠指尖触觉几乎总能成功抓取日常物体，但机器人抓取研究多依赖视觉与平行夹爪，忽视了高分辨率动态触觉在灵巧手中的作用。作者希望系统探究触觉传感对抓取稳定性预测与模型引导抓取的贡献，并提供可扩展的数据驱动路径。

Method: 使用配备四个Digit 360触觉传感器的多指机器人手，在200个物体上采集10,000次抓取试验，记录外部视觉、本体感觉与触觉流。基于该数据集训练端到端时序多模态模型，从抓取抬升前的观测预测抬升后稳定性，并对比不同传感模态与编码骨干网络，进行受控输入消融实验。最后将预测器作为在线稳定性门控部署到真实机器人上。

Result: 实验与消融表明，引入触觉、尤其是高分辨率动态触觉，能提升抓取稳定性预测性能；在真机在线门控中，视触觉模型引导的重新抓取相比非触觉门控，使实际执行抬升的成功率提高10.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 丰富的指尖传感与能捕捉触觉动态的时序模型，可在无需显式接触或力建模的情况下支持多指手的学习型抓取，为从触觉经验走向稳定灵巧操作提供了可扩展的数据驱动路径；数据集已公开。

Abstract: Humans can grasp everyday objects with almost perfect success rates using fingertip tactile feedback, yet much of the robotic grasping literature emphasizes vision-based grasp selection with parallel grippers. In this work, we systematically investigate how high-resolution, dynamic tactile sensing contributes to grasp stability prediction and model-guided grasping in dexterous robotic hands. To this end, we collected a dataset of 10,000 grasp trials across 200 objects using a multi-fingered robotic hand equipped with four Digit 360 tactile sensors, recording external vision, proprioception, and tactile streams throughout each grasp. With this dataset, we trained end-to-end temporal multimodal models to predict post-lift stability from pre-lift grasp observations and compared sensing modalities and encoding backbones. Experimental results and controlled input ablations show that incorporating touch, and particularly high-resolution, dynamic touch, improves grasp stability prediction. Finally, we deployed the learned predictor as an online stability gate on the real robot, where visuo-tactile model-guided regrasping improved the success rate among executed lifts by 10.5 percentage points over a non-tactile gate. These results show how rich fingertip sensing and expressive temporal models that capture the dynamics of touch can support learned grasping with multi-fingered hands without explicit contact or force modeling, providing a scalable data-driven path from tactile experience toward stable dexterous manipulation. The dataset is publicly available at https://lasr-lab.github.io/dexterous-grasp-stability/.

</details>


### [229] [Energy-Efficient Gait Adaptation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10297)
*Ammar Issa,Anubhav Singh,Anton Tsaritsin,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层强化学习框架，将高频关节运动控制与低频步态自适应解耦，显式优化运输成本(CoT)，实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，并在Unitree AlienGo上验证其在多速度、多地形下的高能效与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人运动控制中，能效与鲁棒性难以兼顾，尤其在宽速度范围和复杂地形下。端到端RL策略将步态生成、运动执行与能耗优化耦合在一起，对奖励设计高度敏感，导致能效与鲁棒性难以同时保证。

Method: 提出分层强化学习(HRL)框架：高频策略负责稳定鲁棒的关节级运动执行，低频策略负责步态自适应并显式最小化运输成本(CoT)。采用基于Isaac的三阶段训练流程，实现零样本仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 学习到的分层策略展现自动的速度相关步态自适应：低速时采用踱步(pacing)，高速时切换为小跑(trotting)。在仿真中与单策略及分层运动基线对比，在宽指令速度范围内降低CoT，并在平地、崎岖不平及倾斜地形上保持鲁棒运动。在实体Unitree AlienGo四足机器人上完成零样本部署验证。

Conclusion: 分层强化学习框架通过解耦运动执行与步态自适应，能够在宽速度范围和多地形条件下同时提升能效、跟踪精度与鲁棒性，并具备零样本实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: While energy efficiency is a critical objective for legged-robot locomotion control, achieving low energy consumption while maintaining robust performance across different velocity ranges and terrain conditions remains a key challenge. This is particularly true for end-to-end RL policies, where gait generation, motion execution, and energy optimization are tightly coupled, leading to high sensitivity to reward design. In this work, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) framework that separates a high-frequency policy for stable and robust joint-level motion execution from low-frequency gait adaptation that explicitly minimizes the cost of transport (CoT). The three-stage Isaac-based training procedure enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer with improved tracking accuracy, robustness, and energy efficiency. The learned hierarchy exhibits automatic speed-dependent gait adaptation, transitioning from pacing at low speeds to trotting at higher speeds. We validate the proposed approach in simulation against representative single-policy and hierarchical locomotion baselines, demonstrating reduced CoT over a broad range of commanded velocities, while maintaining robust locomotion across flat, uneven rough, and inclined terrains. We further demonstrate its practical feasibility through zero-shot deployment on a physical Unitree AlienGo quadruped.

</details>


### [230] [Hall Effect-Based Tactile Force Detection Sensor for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10346)
*Charles DeLorey,Michael Brockdorff,Adam Metcalf,Benjamin Calmé,Pietro Valdastri,Dominic Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于霍尔效应的小型触觉力传感器，含三个毫米级传感单元，可贴附于机器人手术器械抓取面，实现法向与剪切力的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人微创手术使外科医生与手术工具机械分离，导致触觉反馈缺失，因此需要能在手术工具上集成的微型力传感器。

Method: 采用霍尔效应传感器与嵌入可变形弹性体接触层的磁体，设计紧凑的三单元（taxel）传感器；通过6自由度并联机器人进行标定。

Result: 每个传感单元可检测法向与剪切力，平均RMSE为2.133 kPa；在体模及离体猪组织上验证，成功实现牵拉与滑动过程中的触觉感知。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种小型霍尔效应触觉传感器，可为力敏感的机器人手术应用提供触觉感知能力。

Abstract: Robotic minimally invasive surgery has revolutionized surgical practice. However, as the surgeons are mechanically separated from the surgical tools, loss of tactile feedback occurs. We present a compact force sensor with three, millimeter scale sensing elements (taxels) that can be affixed to the grasping face of a robotic surgical tool. The sensor features a miniaturized design using Hall effect sensors and magnets embedded in a deformable elastomer contact layer. Sensor calibration is achieved with a 6 degree of freedom (DoF) parallel robot. Each taxel can detect applied normal and shear forces with average RMSE of 2.133 kPa. The calibrated sensor was validated on phantom and ex vivo porcine tissue, demonstrating tactile sensing during retraction as well as slip. This work introduces a small-scale Hall effect based tactile sensor, enabling sensing for force-sensitive robotic surgical applications.

</details>


### [231] [MultiFly: A Real-World Multimodal Aerial Dataset with Annotation-Efficient Label Transfer and Cross-Modal Semantic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10359)
*Markus Gross,Andreas Greiner,Taehyoung Kim,Sivasubiramaniam Subbiah,Tomaž Cotič,Sai Bharadwaj Matha,Conrad Christoph,Oussema Dhaouadi,Simon Zieher,Surya Vijaya Kumar,Gordon Elger,Henri Meeß,Olaf Wysocki,Paul Spannaus,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MultiFly是首个公开的真实世界低空无人机四模态（RGB、热成像、LiDAR、雷达）语义感知数据集，通过几何传播实现高效标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空感知数据集多局限于单一或少数模态，且逐模态标注成本高、一致性差，缺乏同时具备一致逐帧语义标注的多模态低空基准。

Method: 构建含17,272个同步样本、覆盖四个郊区场景、15类语义的数据集，提供标定与GNSS-RTK/IMU测量；仅手工标注115张RGB图像，借助共享几何表示将标签传播至四种模态，生成其余RGB、热成像、LiDAR（840M点）和雷达（3.4M点）标注；并为四模态建立语义分割基准。

Result: 迁移标注与留出手工标注的平均一致率达89.93%，六组模态对的平均语义一致性为90.94%；基准实验揭示了密集LiDAR与稀疏雷达数据的不同架构行为。

Conclusion: MultiFly为多模态航空感知提供了可扩展基础，并据称是首个公开的真实世界低空四模态一致逐帧语义标注基准。

Abstract: We introduce MultiFly, a real-world, low-altitude UAV dataset for semantic perception across RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar modalities. MultiFly provides 17,272 synchronized samples from four suburban scenes with frame-wise annotations for 15 semantic classes, together with calibration and GNSS-RTK/IMU measurements. To avoid costly and inconsistent modality-specific annotation, we propagate labels from only 115 manually annotated RGB images through shared geometric representations to all four modalities. This approach generates semantic labels for 17,157 additional RGB images, 17,272 thermal images, 840M LiDAR points, and 3.4M radar points. Transferred annotations achieve 89.93% average agreement with held-out manual annotations, and 90.94% average semantic consistency across all six modality pairs. We further establish semantic segmentation benchmarks for all four modalities, revealing distinct architectural behavior for dense LiDAR and sparse radar data. Taken together, MultiFly provides a scalable foundation for multimodal aerial perception and, to the best of our knowledge, the first public real-world low-altitude aerial benchmark that combines consistent frame-wise semantic annotations for RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar. Data at https://github.com/markus-42/multifly.

</details>


### [232] [Semantic-Aware Predictive Mapping for Exploration and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10382)
*Kenneth J. K. Ong,William W. J. Teo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过在部分占用地图中注入门引起的歧义，比较纯几何模型与带语义门通道的模型，发现门区域预测精度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性建图可支持机器人探索与导航，但仅用占用表示难以区分几何相似而语义不同的结构，例如室内门在观测中与墙同为占用格，却暗示后方可能存在连通的房间或走廊。因此本文探究语义门线索能否改善这类歧义区域周围的预测性几何占用建图。

Method: 修改 CogniPlan 数据集的子集，在保持真值布局不变的前提下，向部分占用地图中注入由门引起的歧义；构建几何-only 对照模型与语义线索模型，后者在同一修改数据集上训练并额外接收一个门通道；评估指标为全图及 10 像素门区域掩码上的 L1 误差、F1 与 IoU。

Result: 全图性能在两模型间大体相近，但门区域局部结果出现明显定性改善：L1 从 0.004342 降至 0.000025，F1 从 0.031311 升至 1.000000，IoU 从 0.015905 升至 1.000000。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在几何观测本身存在歧义的区域，语义线索能够提升预测性占用补全的效果。

Abstract: Predictive mapping can support robotic exploration and navigation by estimating unseen geometric layouts from partial occupancy observations. However, occupancy-only representations may fail to distinguish semantically different structures with similar geometry. This is particularly relevant for indoor doors, which may appear as occupied cells like walls but indicate possible connected rooms or corridors beyond the observed region. This work investigates whether semantic door cues improve predictive geometric occupancy mapping around such ambiguous regions. We modify a subset of the CogniPlan dataset by inserting door-induced ambiguities into partial occupancy maps while keeping the ground-truth layouts unchanged. We compare a geometry-only control model with a semantic-cued model trained on the same modified dataset, where the semantic-cued model receives an additional door channel. Evaluation uses L1 error, F1 score, and Intersection over Union (IoU) over both the full map and a 10-pixel door-region mask. Full-map performance remains broadly similar between models, but localized door-region results show a clear qualitative improvement: L1 decreases from 0.004342 to 0.000025, while F1 and IoU improve from 0.031311 and 0.015905 to 1.000000 and 1.000000, respectively. These results suggest that semantic cues can improve predictive occupancy completion in regions where geometric observations alone are ambiguous.

</details>


### [233] [OpenViTac: Learning and Benchmarking Visuo-Tactile Policies in a Unified Sim-and-Real Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10384)
*Yifan Wu,Qin Li,Nan Min,Guojin Zhong,Haoyu Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Houze Xu,Shengqi Xu,Xingyao Lin,Zijie Diao,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Shunlin Lu,Shihao Zhao,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenViTac——一个跨仿真与真实世界的视触觉机器人操作基准，并在此基础上提出触觉增强框架 OpenVTLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈能为具身智能体提供超越视觉的物理信息，但视觉-触觉-语言-动作（VTLA）策略虽发展迅速，却缺乏跨仿真与真实世界的统一评测基准。

Method: 1）将富接触操作组织为四个触觉相关能力维度；2）提供配对的仿真-真实世界设置以一致评测 VLA、WAM 与 VTLA 策略；3）研究不同触觉表征与融合策略对预训练 VLA 模型的影响；4）提出触觉增强框架 OpenVTLA，融合最优表征与融合策略；5）利用配对基准研究仿真-真实协同训练及跨域策略学习的影响因素。

Result: 建立了可一致评测 VLA、WAM、VTLA 策略的配对仿真-真实基准，识别出最优触觉表征与融合策略，并借助配对设置分析了影响跨域策略学习的关键因素。

Conclusion: OpenViTac 为评测与推进视触觉机器人操作提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Tactile feedback provides embodied agents with physical information beyond visual observations, enabling more reliable interaction with the real world. However, despite the rapid progress of vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) policies, there remains a lack of unified benchmarks for evaluating tactile-enabled robot manipulation across simulation and the real world. To address this gap, we introduce OpenViTac, a visuo-tactile manipulation benchmark for evaluating robot policies across simulation and the real world. OpenViTac organizes contact-rich manipulation into four tactile-relevant capability dimensions and provides paired simulation-real-world settings for consistent evaluation of VLA, WAM, and VTLA policies. Building upon this benchmark, we investigate how different tactile representations and integration strategies affect the performance of pretrained VLA models. Correspondingly, we introduce OpenVTLA, a tactile augmentation framework that combines the best-performing representation and integration strategy. Furthermore, we leverage the paired benchmark setting to study sim-real co-training and analyze factors affecting cross-domain policy learning. Together, OpenViTac provides a unified platform for evaluating and advancing visuo-tactile robot manipulation.

</details>


### [234] [NeRFifyMesh: Optimizing Neural Radiance Fields from Textured Meshes for Robotics Scene Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10387)
*Nillan Nimal,Mahboubeh Asadi,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将已有3D网格模型转换为NeRF表示的新流程，通过采样网格几何与纹理直接生成真值点基辐射场，免去多视角渲染训练，渲染质量与基线相当，并可用于构建统一NeRF场景与碰撞仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域中NeRF作为新式场景表示备受关注，语义建图与仿真等任务常需用多个NeRF模型构建场景，且每个模型对应一个物体；虽然已有大量3D网格资源，但缺少将其快速转为NeRF模型的工具，阻碍了算法快速开发与测试。

Method: 提出一条网格到NeRF的转换流程：对网格几何与纹理进行采样，人工生成基于点的真值辐射场，从而避免基于相机的采样或渲染多视角图像来训练NeRF模型。并进行了大量基准测试，并将该表示用于构建统一NeRF场景与基于提取几何的碰撞仿真。

Result: 大量基准测试表明，本方法可取得与基线相当的渲染质量；通过构建统一NeRF场景并利用提取的几何进行碰撞仿真，验证了该表示的实际应用能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种不依赖多视角渲染的网格到NeRF转换方案，渲染质量与基线可比，并能支持场景组合与碰撞仿真，便于机器人算法的快速开发与测试。

Abstract: In robotics, scene representation plays a pivotal role in understanding and interacting with the environment. The advent of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and its variants, as a novel representation, has opened a new frontier of research. In applications such as semantic mapping and simulation, roboticists aim to build scenes using multiple NeRF models, each representing an object. While extensive datasets of 3D mesh models already exist, there is an urgent need to develop tools to convert these assets to NeRF models for rapid algorithm development and testing. This paper presents a new pipeline for converting existing mesh models to NeRF representations by artificially generating a ground truth point-based radiance field through sampling mesh geometry and texture. This approach alleviates the need for camera-based sampling or rendering multi-view images of the original mesh to train the NeRF model. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that our method yields comparable rendering quality to the baselines. Additionally, the application of this representation is shown by constructing unified NeRF scenes and performing collision simulations with extracted geometry.

</details>


### [235] [RoboQuest: Generalist Physical Agents that Search, Inspect and Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10388)
*Liu Renhang,Navonil Majumder,Tej Deep Pala,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboQuest基准，用于目标导向的具身探索，揭示前沿多模态智能体在主动获取任务相关信息并据此调整行为方面存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究进展使多模态基础模型能在多种操作任务中充当通用智能体，但面对陌生环境时，任务相关信息常常缺失于观测中：需要确定相关物体的位置、检查未观测的属性、或发现陌生工具的效果。因此需要一个评估智能体通过物理交互主动获取任务相关信息能力的基准。

Method: 提出RoboQuest基准，包含十个移动操作任务，围绕三类不确定性：搜索、基于操作的检查、交互式测试。通过统一的视觉运动接口评估五个前沿多模态智能体，并微调了一个$π_{0.5}$策略（基于所发布的全情节演示）。同时进行执行技能的孤立测试（提供隐藏信息）与失败分析。

Result: 最佳智能体仅在23%的情节中成功，微调后的策略几乎从不成功。孤立执行技能测试（给定隐藏信息）显示智能体大多能完成所需动作，失败分析表明仅有少数失败归因于执行。失败分析还发现：智能体常在观察到完成任务所需证据之前就过早停止探索；很少预防或修复探索带来的干扰；且多数模型难以通过试错学习。

Conclusion: 当前前沿多模态智能体在目标导向的具身探索上能力薄弱，主要瓶颈不在于执行技能，而在于主动信息获取、证据驱动的决策时机、以及对探索干扰的处理，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models have made them capable generalist physical agents for a range of manipulation tasks. However, successful operation in an unfamiliar environment may require an agent to seek task-relevant information through interaction when it is absent from the observations: it may need to determine where a relevant object is, inspect an unobserved property, or discover the effect of an unfamiliar tool. We thus introduce RoboQuest, a benchmark for goal-directed embodied exploration, where agents must actively acquire task-relevant information through physical interaction, use the resulting evidence to adapt subsequent actions, and autonomously decide when to commit to task completion. RoboQuest comprises ten mobile manipulation tasks centered on three forms of uncertainty: search, manipulation-based inspection, and interactive testing. We evaluate five frontier multimodal agents through a common visuomotor interface, as well as a $π_{0.5}$ policy fine-tuned on the full-episode demonstrations we release. The best agent succeeds in only 23\% of the episodes, and the fine-tuned policy almost never succeeds. Isolated tests of the execution skills the tasks are built from, with the hidden information supplied, show that the agents can carry out most of the required actions, and our failure analysis attributes only a minority of the failures to execution. Our failure analysis further finds that the agents often stop exploring too early as they make decisions before observing the required evidence for task completion. We also find that agents rarely prevent or repair the disturbances caused by their exploration. Moreover, learning by trial and error remains difficult for most models.

</details>


### [236] [RobotWorld: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents for Robot Use Across Diverse Tasks and Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10409)
*Zhiqin Yang,Chenxin Li,Xiaomeng Hu,Yibin Liu,Weidong Huang,Jiankai Sun,Haitao Li,Zijian Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Fanding Huang,Hanwen Sun,Jiashun Liu,Jingqi Tong,Mingxin Huang,Shaoli Hu,Shijue Huang,Tianyi Bai,Xinyuan Wang,Yunlong Lin,Zhengyang Tang,Zhexin Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Xierui Song,Juntao Dai,Boyuan Chen,Jiaming Ji,Fangneng Zhan,Mengkang Hu,Wei Xue,Yonggang Zhang,Han Hu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RobotWorld，一个将通用智能体的代码与工具能力延伸到物理世界的仿真测试平台，包含 84 项机器人任务（操作、移动操作、运动、驾驶、空中控制），带交互预算与可执行成功判定，并通过执行轨迹分析揭示能力迁移的不均匀性及具体缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体日益擅长写代码、用工具、完成复杂数字任务，但这些能力能在多大程度上迁移到物理世界尚不清楚，缺乏严格的评测基准来刻画其能力边界与失败模式。

Method: 构建 RobotWorld 仿真测试平台：将指令与观测经机器人接口转化为物理任务执行；覆盖 5 类共 84 项任务，设置显式交互预算和可执行的成功检查；结合任务结果与执行轨迹进行联合分析。

Result: 智能体能够构建复杂的感知与控制流程（图像分割、相机标定、空间估计、基于动力学的计算），但这些能力无法稳定组合为成功行为：到达指令位姿却丢失任务相关物体状态、无法纠正无效动作、恢复过晚、把未完成任务误判为完成。迁移表现也因模型而异：Astra 在空间与受限接触目标上更成功，Opus 5.5 在持续平衡与定时交互目标上更成功。

Conclusion: 通过将任务结果与执行行为相联系，RobotWorld 既提供了严格的验证平台，也给出了关于剩余能力缺口的实证分析，为训练和设计更可靠的物理世界智能体确立了具体目标。

Abstract: General-purpose agents increasingly write code, use tools, and complete complex digital tasks, raising the question of how far these capabilities carry into the physical world. To investigate this, we introduce RobotWorld, a challenging simulation testbed for robot use: turning instructions and observations into physical task execution through robot interfaces. Its 84 tasks span manipulation, mobile manipulation, locomotion, driving, and aerial control, with explicit interaction budgets and executable success checks. By analysing task outcomes alongside execution traces, we identify both the capabilities that transfer and the gaps that prevent reliable completion. Furthermore, we find that current agents can construct sophisticated perception and control workflows, including image segmentation, camera calibration, spatial estimation, and dynamics-based computation. These capabilities, however, do not consistently compose into successful behaviour: agents lose task-relevant object states despite reaching commanded poses, fail to correct ineffective actions, recover too late, or mistake unfinished tasks for completion. This uneven transfer also differs across models: Astra succeeds more often on spatial and constrained-contact goals, whereas Opus 5.5 succeeds more often on continuous-balance and timed-interaction goals. By linking these outcomes to execution behaviour, RobotWorld provides both a rigorous proving ground and an empirical account of the remaining capability gaps, thereby establishing concrete targets for training and designing more reliable physical-world agents.

</details>


### [237] [AirGroundVLN: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Goal-Oriented Air-Ground Collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10421)
*Zhenxuan Zeng,Qingle Wu,Wei Suo,Maojia Wu,Bairong Zhang,Hangzheng Yu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出空地协同的目标准向视觉语言导航基准AirGroundVLN与参考框架AG-CoNAV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标准向空地协同VLN缺乏大规模多样基准，且面临跨平台跨时间空间上下文一致性差、空地感知不对称导致单平台规划不可靠两大挑战。

Method: 构建含10,281个导航回合、955个目标实例、19个Unreal Engine环境的AirGroundVLN基准（含已见/未见划分与空中可见性协议）；提出AG-CoNAV框架，含时空锚定协同记忆SACM与空中引导区域到局部规划AGRLP。

Result: 大量实验验证AG-CoNAV有效性，并确立AirGroundVLN为综合性基准。

Conclusion: AG-CoNAV有效，AirGroundVLN为未来空地协同VLN研究提供综合基准平台。

Abstract: Goal-oriented Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to locate and reach targets described in natural language without prescribed routes. Air--ground collaboration is valuable for tasks requiring both wide-area search and fine-grained localization. However, systematic study of goal-oriented air--ground collaborative VLN remains limited by the lack of large-scale, diverse benchmarks and two core challenges: 1) substantial differences between aerial and ground views, together with useful observations becoming unavailable as navigation proceeds, make it difficult to maintain spatially consistent context across platforms and over time; and 2) asymmetric spatial observability makes ground perception locally detailed but spatially limited and aerial perception broad but locally coarse, limiting the reliability of single-platform planning. To address these limitations, we introduce AirGroundVLN, a benchmark containing 10,281 navigation episodes and 955 target instances across 19 Unreal Engine environments, with seen/unseen splits and an aerial-visibility protocol for systematic evaluation. Alongside the benchmark, we propose AG-CoNAV, a trainable reference framework comprising two key components: Spatiotemporally Anchored Collaborative Memory (SACM) and Aerial-Guided Regional-to-Local Planning (AGRLP). SACM maintains and retrieves spatially consistent historical context across aerial and ground observations. Meanwhile, AGRLP combines regional aerial guidance with fine-grained ground navigation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of AG-CoNAV and establish AirGroundVLN as a comprehensive benchmark for future exploration.

</details>


### [238] [RFPO: Rectified Flow Policy Optimization for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10453)
*Ting Huang,Lisiyu Pan,Haoyu Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RFPO 提出一种面向可靠少步执行的流策略优化框架，通过奖励感知的在线 Reflow 与冻结高斯 PPO 控制器协同监督，使单步欧拉执行的流学生策略在多种机器人上保持接近全步执行的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的策略表达能力强，可用于连续机器人控制，但其迭代式 ODE 积分带来很高的推理成本。简单减少积分步数会显著降低控制性能，因为在全步执行下优化的策略并未被显式约束为在粗粒度数值积分下仍可靠，作者将此不匹配称为“少步离散化差距”。

Method: 提出 RFPO 流策略优化框架。核心包括：奖励感知的在线 Reflow，在 on-policy 学习过程中修正学生策略诱导的传输路径，使其对粗粒度积分更鲁棒；同时使用冻结的高斯 PPO 控制器在全步与中间积分预算下提供动作空间监督；最终部署策略仍为单个流学生，且仅用一步欧拉执行。

Result: 在 Unitree Go2、Boston Dynamics Spot、Unitree H1、Unitree G1 上，RFPO 在单步执行下持续保持全步控制性能；在零初始化与随机初始化下，单步回报均保持在对应 64 步值的 2.4% 以内。在 Unitree Go2 上，单步执行保留 64 步奖励的 98.5%，同时将板载平均推理延迟从 4.39 ms 降至 0.08 ms，实现 54.9 倍加速。真实机器人实验进一步验证了稳定的单步运动控制。

Conclusion: RFPO 有效弥合了少步离散化差距，证明通过奖励感知在线 Reflow 与高斯 PPO 协同监督，可以让流策略以单步执行获得接近全步执行的控制性能与显著推理加速，并在真实机器人上实现稳定运动。

Abstract: Flow-based policies provide an expressive framework for continuous robot control, but their iterative ODE integration incurs substantial inference cost. Naively reducing the integration budget can severely degrade control, since policies optimized under full-step execution are not explicitly constrained to remain reliable under coarse numerical integration. We refer to this mismatch as the few-step discretization gap. To address this problem, we introduce RFPO, a flow-policy optimization framework for reliable few-step execution. Reward-aware online Reflow rectifies student-induced transport paths during on-policy learning, making the resulting policy more robust to coarse integration. A frozen Gaussian PPO controller supplies complementary action-space supervision at full and intermediate integration budgets, while the deployed policy remains a single flow student executed with one Euler step. Across Unitree Go2, Boston Dynamics Spot, Unitree H1, and Unitree G1, RFPO consistently preserves full-step control performance under one-step execution, with one-step returns remaining within 2.4% of their corresponding 64-step values across both zero and random initialization. On Unitree Go2, one-step execution retains 98.5% of the 64-step reward while reducing onboard mean inference latency from 4.39 ms to 0.08 ms, yielding a 54.9x speedup. Real-robot experiments further validate stable one-step locomotion. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/RFPO. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/RFPO.

</details>


### [239] [FoldBack: Self-Correcting Masked Generative Policy for Long-Horizon Garment Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10462)
*Lipeng Zhuang,Shiyu Fan,Yingdong Ru,Zhuo He,Florent P. Audonnet,Paul Henderson,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoldBack是一种用于长程衣物折叠的自校正掩码生成策略，通过推理时的精炼验证、回滚与选择性重试机制，在无需恢复示范或重训练的情况下显著提升折叠成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程轨迹策略在抓取遗漏或滑脱后仍会继续执行，即使衣物未达到目标配置，导致失败累积。论文旨在让失败的交互能在执行继续之前被检测、撤销和修复。

Method: FoldBack围绕三个推理时决策构建恢复机制：何时精炼与验证、如何回滚、以及在哪里及如何重试。它将精炼与抓取验证对齐到抓放事件，将机器人带回可重试的预抓取配置并保留成功抓取，同时选择性重新生成失败片段及选定的未来动作，并避开此前失败的抓取位置。

Result: 在来自六个类别的33件真实衣物上，FoldBack实现了75.2%的最终折叠成功率和0.837的最终掩码IoU，而最强先前基线分别为45.7%和0.689。

Conclusion: FoldBack是首个统一上述决策的可编辑全轨迹策略，无需恢复示范或基础策略重训练即可实现失败交互的检测、撤销与修复，显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: We present FoldBack, a self-correcting masked generative policy for long-horizon garment folding. Existing long-trajectory policies may continue after a missed or slipped grasp even when the garment has not reached the intended configuration. We structure FoldBack's recovery mechanisms around three inference-time decisions: when to refine and verify, how to roll back, and where and how to retry. FoldBack aligns refinement and grasp verification with pick-and-place events, returns the robot to a retryable pre-grasp configuration while preserving successful grasps, and selectively regenerates the failed segment and selected future actions while avoiding previous failed grasp locations. To our knowledge, FoldBack is the first editable full-trajectory policy to unify these decisions, enabling failed interactions to be detected, undone, and repaired before execution continues, without recovery demonstrations or base-policy retraining. Across 33 real garments from six categories, FoldBack achieves 75.2% final folding success and 0.837 final-mask IoU, versus 45.7% and 0.689 for the strongest prior baseline.

</details>


### [240] [LLA-MPPI: Rapidly Adaptive Whole-body Control of Legged Robots with GPU-Accelerated Parallel Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10465)
*Sebin Jung,Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Juan Alvarez-Padilla,Zachary Manchester,Changliu Liu,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LLA-MPPI 方法，通过窗口化预测误差从 GPU 批量接触仿真器库中选取最匹配当前动力学的模型，实现腿足机器人全身自适应控制，无需离线训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时全身控制器通常基于固定标称模型，当实际动力学变化时性能下降；自适应方法要么需要接触动力学无法提供的模型结构，要么需针对每种预设工况离线训练。

Method: 将全身自适应转化为对一组具有不同物理或结构参数的 GPU 批量接触仿真器的选择：用滑动窗口的预测误差选出最能解释近期运动的仿真器，再由全身 MPPI 规划器通过所选模型优化控制量。框架无需离线训练，所选假设具有物理可解释性。

Result: 在四个仿真任务中达到 97.5% 成功率，最强基线为 74%，使用真实模型的神谕为 98.5%。在 Unitree Go2 硬件上验证了中途加载载荷仍可行走、单腿失效后可行走、以及推动箱子时实时增加其质量。

Conclusion: LLA-MPPI 为腿足机器人全身自适应控制提供了无需离线训练、物理可解释且接近神谕性能的实时方案，并在真实硬件上验证了对动力学变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-time whole-body controllers for legged robots typically plan through a fixed nominal model and degrade when the deployed dynamics change. Adaptive methods typically require a model structure that contact dynamics do not provide, or they need offline training for each anticipated condition. We present Look-back and Look-ahead Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral control (LLA-MPPI). The method converts whole-body adaptation into selection over a bank of GPU-batched contact simulators with different physical or structural parameters. Windowed prediction errors select the simulator that best explains recent motion. A whole-body MPPI planner optimizes controls through the selected model. The framework requires no offline training, and its selected hypotheses are physically interpretable. Across four simulated tasks, it achieves 97.5% success while the strongest baseline reaches 74% and an oracle with the true model reaches 98.5%. Hardware validation on a Unitree Go2 shows the robot walking under a payload added mid-run, walking after one leg is disabled, and pushing a box to its goal while increasing its mass on the fly. Code, videos, and project details are available at: https://lla-control.github.io

</details>


### [241] [CMP-IRRT*: A Perception-Assisted Height-Adaptive Planner for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10470)
*Mingfan Zhao,Wendong Mao,Zhongfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMP-IRRT*：一种基于Channel Mamba PointNet引导的Informed RRT*高度自适应规划框架，利用俯视RGB感知估计障碍与相对高度图，实现“低障碍可跨越、高障碍视为阻挡”的高度条件碰撞检测，并引导采样，提升规划效率与路径质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人能够跨越低矮障碍，但许多2D规划流程仍将障碍建模为二值占据区域，并依赖采样搜索，在有限预算下效率低下。

Method: 1) 感知：基于标定的俯视RGB观测，估计障碍区域并将深度预测转换为地面相对高度图；2) 规划：基于CMP-IRRT*（Channel Mamba PointNet引导的Informed RRT*），进行高度条件碰撞检测，高障碍视为不可通行、低障碍允许跨越；3) 采样：使用CMP引导采样偏向有希望区域，同时保留标准自由空间采样与informed采样作为回退。

Result: 在2D规划基准上，CMP-IRRT*相比经典与神经引导基线减少探索节点数与迭代次数；消融实验支持Mamba引导的贡献。在构建的可通行性感知场景中，当低障碍可跨越时路径长度最多减少16.3%；Unitree Go2演示展示了可执行的绕行与跨越行为。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 该框架将感知得到的高度信息与引导采样结合，使四足机器人在规划中区分可跨越与不可跨越障碍，从而提升有限预算下的规划效率和路径质量，并具备真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Quadruped robots can traverse low obstacles, but many 2D planning pipelines still model obstacles as binary occupied regions and rely on sampling-based search that can be inefficient under a limited budget. We propose a perception-assisted height-adaptive planning framework based on CMP-IRRT*, a Channel Mamba PointNet-guided Informed RRT* planner. Given a calibrated top-view RGB observation, the perception module estimates obstacle regions and converts depth predictions into a ground-relative height map. The planner then performs height-conditioned collision checking, treating high obstacles as blocked while allowing low obstacles to be traversed, and uses the CMP guide to bias sampling toward promising regions while retaining standard free-space and informed sampling fallbacks. Experiments on 2D planning benchmarks show that CMP-IRRT* reduces explored nodes and iterations compared with classical and neural-guided baselines, and a controlled ablation supports the contribution of the Mamba-based guide. In constructed traversability-aware scenarios, the proposed planner reduces path length by up to 16.3% when low obstacles are traversable, and a Unitree Go2 demonstration further shows executable bypassing and traversal behaviors. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/MingfanZhao/height-adaptive-planner.

</details>


### [242] [Robotic Boomerang Throwing via Model-Based Release Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10472)
*Yang Liu,Colin Jones,Aude Billard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的机器人回旋镖投掷框架，以释放状态为核心，实现6自由度机械臂首次完成回旋镖返回飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投掷具有气动升力的物体可大幅扩展机器人投掷能力，回旋镖飞行对释放速度、姿态和旋转高度敏感，而机械臂难以复现人类投掷的快速动作，因此需要专门的建模与设计方法。

Method: 分阶段辨识回旋镖飞行动力学以预测设计变化下的飞行；筛选候选参数，评估其在不确定接触条件下对释放旋转的控制强度与一致性；据此设计投掷动作与回旋镖，适配关节速度受限的6自由度机械臂。

Result: 首次实现机械臂生成回旋镖返回飞行；示范试验中释放转速51 rad/s（8.1 rev/s），最远达距基座2.03 m，返回触地点距基座0.31 m；成功释放与人类投掷实测差异显著。

Conclusion: 机器人无需模仿人类投掷动作即可实现回旋镖返回飞行，验证了以释放状态为中心的模型框架的有效性。

Abstract: Throwing objects that generate aerodynamic lift can greatly extend robot throwing beyond ballistic flight. A returning boomerang is a challenging example because its flight depends strongly on the release velocity, attitude, and spin, while robotic manipulators cannot readily reproduce the rapid motions used in human throwing. We present a model-based framework for robotic boomerang throwing centered on the release state. We identify the boomerang flight dynamics in stages to predict how flight changes across design variations. To systematically design the robot throwing motion, we screen candidate parameters according to how strongly and consistently they control release spin under uncertain contact conditions. These models are then used to design the throwing motion and boomerang for a 6-DoF manipulator with limited joint speeds. To our knowledge, this is the first robotic manipulator to generate a returning boomerang flight. In the demonstrated returning trial, the boomerang is released at 51 rad/s (8.1 rev/s), reaches 2.03 m from the robot base, and returns to touch down 0.31 m from the base. The successful release differs significantly from the measured human throws, showing that a robot need not imitate human throwing motion to achieve a returning flight. The project page is available at https://robot-boomerang.github.io

</details>


### [243] [Agentic RSR: Real-to-Sim-to-Real through Scene Reconstruction and Execution-Grounded Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10479)
*Yihan Li,Yating Feng,Shengjiu Sun,Jianing Chen,Hao Ren,Bowen Yang,Weisheng Xu,Qiwei Wu,Hui Cheng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic RSR 框架，将场景重建、策略开发与真机执行通过同一操作任务串联，实现从视频到可执行策略的 real-to-sim-to-real 流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人工作区的仿真需保留任务相关交互，且仿真中开发的策略必须基于真机可用的观测。然而现有方法通常将场景重建与策略开发分开处理，导致仿真与真机脱节。

Method: 给定工作区视频、任务描述和已知机器人模型，智能体恢复度量尺度，利用视觉反馈迭代精化场景，并在 MuJoCo 中检查任务相关交互；随后编码智能体开发可执行策略，从特权物体位姿逐步过渡到视觉观测与随机化仿真；策略可在单次调用中交错多观测与动作，并依据执行反馈继续、重试或修正；通过共享任务级接口将策略与累积经验迁移至真机，并由新观测与安全检查指导执行。

Result: 在 18 个重建场景（两种机器人）中，四视角深度 MAE 均值 0.1057 m，Lab ΔE76 均值 11.04，灰度 SSIM 均值 0.6990；真机任务总体成功率达成仿真成功率的 80%。

Conclusion: 该框架将重建、策略开发与部署统一在同一任务下，使仿真性能在硬件上得到较大程度保留，代码与重建场景数据将公开。

Abstract: A simulation of a real robot workspace must preserve task-relevant interactions, while policies developed in it must operate on observations available to the real robot. Yet scene reconstruction and policy development are often treated separately. We present Agentic Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Agentic RSR), a framework that links scene reconstruction, policy development, and real-robot execution through the same manipulation task. Given a workspace video, a task description, and a known robot model, an agent recovers metric scale, iteratively refines the scene using visual feedback, and checks task-relevant interactions in MuJoCo. A coding agent then develops an executable policy, progressing from privileged object poses to visual observations and randomized simulation. The policy can interleave multiple observations and actions within one invocation, while the agent uses execution feedback to continue, retry, or revise its approach. A shared task-level interface carries the policy and accumulated experience to the real robot, where fresh observations and safety checks guide execution. Across 18 reconstructed scenes involving two robots, the mean four-view Depth MAE against reference depth estimates is 0.1057 m, the mean Lab $ΔE_{76}$ is 11.04, and the mean grayscale SSIM is 0.6990. In real-robot experiments, the aggregate task success rate reaches 80% of the simulation task success rate, indicating substantial retention of simulated performance on hardware. Code and reconstructed scene data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [244] [HuMBLE: Human Motion-Driven Behavior Learning for Embodied Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10489)
*Mike Zhang,Dongho Kang,Kevin Bergamin,Nicola Burger,Robin Deits,Jonathan Foster,Bilal Hammoud,Katie Hughes,Francesco Iacobelli,Twan Koolen,M. Eva Mungai,Zach Nobles,Shane Rozen-Levy,Jean Pierre Sleiman,Fangzhou Yu,Yunbo Zhang,Alfred Rizzi,Jessica Hodgins,Scott Kuindersma,Yeuhi Abe,Sylvain Bertrand,Farbod Farshidian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个学习框架，通过“教师-学生蒸馏 + 多任务微调”从人类运动数据中合成实时可控、鲁棒且仿生的类人机器人步态策略，并在Atlas R1/D1与Unitree G1上完成实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人机器人运动控制存在两难：以指令跟踪和鲁棒性为目标的控制器步态机械生硬；而依赖人类运动数据的控制器又难以泛化到数据分布之外的指令。作者希望在两者之间取得平衡，获得既仿生又鲁棒、可实时转向的策略。

Method: 首先构建覆盖多速度、多方向的自采运动数据集；用强化学习训练一个以全身参考为条件的策略，再通过教师-学生蒸馏将其压缩为仅依赖本体感受和平面躯干速度转向指令的轻量先验策略；随后用多任务强化学习微调该先验策略，任务包括跟踪任意指令的目标条件任务和以人类数据为显式风格正则项的参考引导任务，从而在数据分布之外扩展指令覆盖与鲁棒性。

Result: 在Boston Dynamics Atlas R1、Atlas D1和Unitree G1三种类人机器人上验证：在室内外真实场景中实现用户直接操控的运动，并能作为分层控制栈中的运动层集成。与不使用人类数据训练的Tabula Rasa强化学习策略的对比及消融实验表明，该方法产出的轻量可部署策略能仅凭转向指令重建协调的全身行为，在保持人类步态特征的同时兼具鲁棒性与完全可操控性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功调和了指令跟踪/鲁棒性与仿人性之间的冲突，实现从人类数据出发的实时、可控、鲁棒且仿生的类人运动策略，并具备跨多平台实机部署的实用性。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in humanoid locomotion, controllers optimized for command tracking and robustness tend to produce mechanical gaits, whereas controllers tied to human motion data often fail to generalize to commands outside the data distribution. This work introduces a learning framework that balances these competing objectives to synthesize real-time steerable, robust, and biomimetic locomotion policies from human data. Using an in-house curated locomotion dataset covering diverse speeds and directions, we first learn a natural locomotion prior policy through a teacher-student distillation process. Specifically, we train a full-body reference-conditioned policy with Reinforcement Learning (RL), then distill it into a lightweight prior policy conditioned solely on proprioception and a planar torso-velocity steering command. Next, we fine-tune the prior policy with multi-task RL to expand command coverage and robustness beyond the data distribution, pairing a goal-conditioned task that tracks arbitrary commands with a reference-guided task that tracks the human data as an explicit style regularizer. We validate our framework on three humanoid robots: the Boston Dynamics Atlas R1, Atlas D1, and Unitree G1. Experimental results demonstrate robust performance across real-world scenarios, including direct user-controlled locomotion in indoor and outdoor environments, and integration as the locomotion layer within hierarchical control stacks. Benchmarks against Tabula Rasa RL policies trained without human data and ablation studies confirm that our framework yields a lightweight, deployable policy that reconstructs coordinated whole-body behavior from a steering command, retaining the human gait characteristics while remaining robust and fully steerable.

</details>


### [245] [Factorized Tactile Representation and Control for Sim-to-Real Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10510)
*Siqi Shang,Bianca Aumann,Tye Brady,Joshua Migdal,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分解式触觉表征与控制框架，将有效接触响应分解为接触几何、力分布和时序接触变化，配合触觉门控策略实现仿真到现实迁移，在接触定位、力跟踪和真实对抗性插销任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉仿真到现实学习需弥合仿真接触与设备相关传感器响应之间的差距，同时保留控制所需信息；现有方法未区分不同触觉表征的作用，难以独立评估其效用与迁移可靠性。

Method: 提出因子化触觉表征与控制框架，将法向力与接触面片映射为可从传感器读数恢复的有效接触响应；响应分离为接触几何、力分布与时序接触变化，并针对每种表征采用专门的编码与随机化；采用触觉门控策略（Tactile Gated Policy）在控制过程中分别保留这些表征，并可在所有掩码配置下无需重训练运行。

Result: 通过响应重建、空间对齐、力调节及仿真与真实世界的接触密集型对抗性插销实验进行评测；实现小于1 mm的接触定位精度、在未见几何上1.69 N的力跟踪误差，真实世界对抗插销较未分解响应提升35%，且不同触觉表征对不同交互各有优势。

Conclusion: 因子化触觉表征可独立评估各类触觉表征的效用与迁移可靠性，有效提升触觉仿真到现实的控制性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Tactile sim-to-real learning must bridge simulated contact and device-specific sensor responses while preserving information needed for control. We propose a factorized tactile representation and control framework that maps normal force and contact patch to an effective contact response recoverable from sensor readings. The response is separated into contact geometry, force distribution, and temporal contact change, with representation-specific encoding and randomization. A Tactile Gated Policy preserves these representations separately through control and operates over all mask configurations without retraining. We evaluate the approach through response reconstruction, spatial alignment, force regulation, and contact-rich adversarial peg insertion in simulation and the real world, enabling the utility and transfer reliability of different tactile representations to be assessed independently. The approach achieves <1 mm contact localization, 1.69 N force-tracking error on unseen geometries, and a 35% improvement in real-world adversarial peg insertion over the unfactorized response, with different tactile representations benefiting different interactions.

</details>
